**-- Begin of change TR#DS1K921478 Def#209100 SKUMAR 01-Feb-2021
  IF fkdat-low IS NOT INITIAL AND fkdat-high IS NOT INITIAL AND sy-batch = abap_false.
    DATA: lv_date TYPE p0001-begda,
          lv_bjob TYPE c.
    CLEAR lv_bjob.
    CALL FUNCTION 'RP_CALC_DATE_IN_INTERVAL'
      EXPORTING
        date      = fkdat-low
        days      = '00'
        months    = '03'
        signum    = '+'
        years     = '00'
      IMPORTING
        calc_date = lv_date. ##FM_SUBRC_OK
    IF fkdat-high GT lv_date AND rb_email IS INITIAL.
*      MESSAGE i368(00) WITH 'Billing Date Range should be' '3 Months only' '' ''.
      MESSAGE i368(00) WITH 'As Billing date range exceeds 90 days,'(105) 'Choose EMAIL option to execute this report'(106) '' ''.
      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ELSEIF fkdat-high GT lv_date AND rb_email IS NOT INITIAL.
      PERFORM process_in_background.
      lv_bjob = abap_true.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
**-- End of change TR#DS1K921478 Def#209100 SKUMAR 01-Feb-2021
  IF lv_bjob IS INITIAL.
    PERFORM fetch_data.
    IF gt_final IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF rb_list IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM build_fc.
        PERFORM display_alv.
      ELSEIF rb_email IS NOT INITIAL.
        g_attachment_name = TEXT-100."'Sales Report'.
        g_subject = TEXT-100."'Sales Report'.
        APPEND TEXT-011  TO g_main_text.
**-- Begin of change TR#DS1K921478 Def#209100 SKUMAR 01-Feb-2021
        PERFORM send_email_attachment USING   gt_final
                                             g_attachment_name
                                             g_subject
                                             g_main_text.
**-- End of change TR#DS1K921478 Def#209100 SKUMAR 01-Feb-2021

      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      MESSAGE 'No Data found for selection date'(108) TYPE 'I'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

FORM fetch_data .
  TABLES : bkpf.
  TYPES : BEGIN OF ty_t001,
            bukrs TYPE t001-bukrs,
            waers TYPE t001-waers,
          END OF ty_t001.
  TYPES : BEGIN OF ty_bkpf,
            bukrs TYPE bkpf-bukrs,
            belnr TYPE bkpf-belnr,
          END OF ty_bkpf,
          ltt_i_cmir TYPE TABLE OF zsotc_i_cmir WITH EMPTY KEY. "TR DS1K925078++ Defect# 213371 GKOTESWARA 29-June-2021

  TYPES: BEGIN OF lty_exchange,
           waerk TYPE vbrk-waerk,
           fkdat TYPE vbrk-fkdat.
           INCLUDE TYPE bapi1093_0.
TYPES END OF lty_exchange.


  DATA : lt_i_cmir   TYPE TABLE OF zsotc_i_cmir,
         lwa_quarter TYPE p99sg_quarter,
         lc_q        TYPE string VALUE 'Q',
         lv_q        TYPE string,
         lwa_i_cmir  TYPE zsotc_i_cmir,
         lt_t001     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_t001,
         lwa_t001    TYPE ty_t001,
         lt_t001_1   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_t001,
         lwa_t001_1  TYPE ty_t001,
         lt_bkpf     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_bkpf,
         ls_bkpf     TYPE ty_bkpf.
  DATA: lwa_exchage     TYPE bapi1093_0,

                          year                = s_fis-low
                        EXCEPTIONS
                          period_in_not_valid = 1
                          period_not_assigned = 2
                          version_undefined   = 3
                          OTHERS              = 4. ##FM_SUBRC_OK
                      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
                      ELSE.
                      ENDIF.
                      APPEND s_per.
                      APPEND s_fis.
                    ENDIF.
                  ENDLOOP.
                  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM s_per COMPARING ALL FIELDS.
                  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM s_fis COMPARING ALL FIELDS.

                  DATA(lt_keph_temp) = lt_keph1[].
                  SORT lt_keph_temp BY kalnr.
                  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_keph_temp COMPARING kalnr.
                  IF lt_keph_temp IS NOT INITIAL.
*CKMLPRKEPH query
                    SELECT kalnr, bdatj, poper, prtyp, kkzst, curtp, kst001, kst003, kst005, kst007, kst009, kst011, kst013, kst017
                           FROM ckmlprkeph
                           INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_ckmlprkeph)
                           FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_keph_temp
                           WHERE kalnr = @lt_keph_temp-kalnr
                             AND bdatj IN @s_fis
                             AND poper IN @s_per
                             AND ( prtyp = @lc_v OR prtyp = @lc_s )
                             AND  ( curtp = @lc_10 OR curtp = @lc_30 ).
                    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
                      DELETE lt_ckmlprkeph WHERE kkzst IS NOT INITIAL.
                      SORT lt_ckmlprkeph BY kalnr curtp.
                    ENDIF.
                  ENDIF.
                ENDIF.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form FILL_PO_RAN_MAN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--P_GWA_FINAL  text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM fill_po_ran_man  USING p_vbeln
                            p_posnr
                      CHANGING p_gwa_final TYPE gty_final. "TR DS1K926255++ Defect 215087 GKOTESWARA 23-Sep-2021
  "zotcsh001_dt."zotcsh001."gty_final_1."zotcsh001.
  DATA: lv_name  TYPE thead-tdname,
        lt_text  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF tline,
        lwa_text TYPE tline.
  CLEAR lv_name.
  CONCATENATE p_vbeln p_posnr INTO lv_name.
* Fill Manifest number
  REFRESH: lt_text.
  CLEAR : lwa_text.
  CALL FUNCTION 'READ_TEXT'
    EXPORTING
      id                      = 'Z011'
      language                = sy-langu
      name                    = lv_name
      object                  = 'VBBP'
    TABLES
      lines                   = lt_text
    EXCEPTIONS
      id                      = 1
      language                = 2
      name                    = 3
      not_found               = 4
      object                  = 5
      reference_check         = 6
      wrong_access_to_archive = 7
      OTHERS                  = 8. ##FM_SUBRC_OK
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    REFRESH lt_text[].
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT lt_text INTO lwa_text.
    CONCATENATE p_gwa_final-manifest_no lwa_text-tdline INTO p_gwa_final-manifest_no SEPARATED BY space.
    SHIFT p_gwa_final-manifest_no LEFT DELETING LEADING ' '.
  ENDLOOP.
* Fille RAN Number
  REFRESH: lt_text.
  CLEAR : lwa_text.
  CALL FUNCTION 'READ_TEXT'
    EXPORTING
      id                      = 'Z006'
      language                = sy-langu
      name                    = lv_name
      object                  = 'VBBP'
    TABLES
      lines                   = lt_text
    EXCEPTIONS
      id                      = 1
      language                = 2
      name                    = 3
      not_found               = 4
      object                  = 5
      reference_check         = 6
      wrong_access_to_archive = 7
      OTHERS                  = 8. ##FM_SUBRC_OK
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    REFRESH lt_text[].
  ENDIF.
  LOOP AT lt_text INTO lwa_text.
    CONCATENATE p_gwa_final-ran lwa_text-tdline INTO p_gwa_final-ran SEPARATED BY space.
    SHIFT p_gwa_final-ran LEFT DELETING LEADING ' '.
  ENDLOOP.
* Fill PO Number
  REFRESH: lt_text.
  CLEAR : lwa_text.
  CALL FUNCTION 'READ_TEXT'
    EXPORTING
      id                      = 'Z008'
      language                = sy-langu
      name                    = lv_name
      object                  = 'VBBP'
    TABLES
      lines                   = lt_text
    EXCEPTIONS
      id                      = 1
      language                = 2
      name                    = 3
      not_found               = 4
      object                  = 5
      reference_check         = 6
      wrong_access_to_archive = 7
      OTHERS                  = 8. ##FM_SUBRC_OK
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    REFRESH lt_text[].
  ENDIF.
  LOOP AT lt_text INTO lwa_text.
    CONCATENATE p_gwa_final-po lwa_text-tdline INTO p_gwa_final-po SEPARATED BY space.
    SHIFT p_gwa_final-po LEFT DELETING LEADING ' '.
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form change_field_headings
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      --> LR_COLS
*&      <-- LR_COLUMN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM change_field_headings  USING    lo_cols TYPE REF TO cl_salv_columns
                            CHANGING lo_column TYPE REF TO cl_salv_column..


  TRY.
      lo_column = lo_cols->get_column( 'VKORG' ).
      lo_column->set_alignment( '3' ).
      lo_column->set_leading_spaces( 'X' ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.
  ENDTRY.
  TRY.
      lo_column = lo_cols->get_column( 'ZVKORG_NAME' ).
      lo_column->set_alignment( '3' ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.
  ENDTRY.

  TRY.
      lo_column = lo_cols->get_column( 'ZWENO' ).
      lo_column->set_alignment( '3' ).
      lo_column->set_leading_spaces( 'X' ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.
  ENDTRY.
  TRY.
      lo_column = lo_cols->get_column( 'ZORDERNO' ).
      lo_column->set_alignment( '3' ).
      lo_column->set_leading_spaces( 'X' ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.
  ENDTRY.

  TRY.
      lo_column = lo_cols->get_column( 'ZLNNO' ).
      lo_column->set_alignment( '3' ).
      lo_column->set_leading_spaces( 'X' ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.
  ENDTRY.
  TRY.
      lo_column = lo_cols->get_column( 'ZCURCODE' ).
      lo_column->set_alignment( '3' ).
      lo_column->set_leading_spaces( 'X' ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.
  ENDTRY.
ENDFORM.

        i_att_content_hex    = binary_content ). "l_xls_content ).

***-- Add Document to send request
      lr_send_request->set_document( l_document ).

      LOOP AT s_email.
        l_c_address = s_email-low.
        l_recipient = cl_cam_address_bcs=>create_internet_address(
          i_address_string = l_c_address ).

        lr_send_request->add_recipient( i_recipient = l_recipient ).
      ENDLOOP.

***-- Send document
      lr_sent_to_all = lr_send_request->send(
        i_with_error_screen = c_x ).

      IF lr_sent_to_all = c_x.
        MESSAGE s022(so).
      ENDIF.

      COMMIT WORK.

    CATCH  cx_bcs INTO lr_bcs_execption.
      WRITE TEXT-001.
      WRITE: TEXT-002, lr_bcs_execption->error_type.
  ENDTRY.

ENDFORM.

*& Form process_in_background
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM process_in_background .

  DATA: lv_job_name         LIKE tbtco-jobname,
        lv_job_nr           LIKE tbtco-jobcount,
        lv_job_released     TYPE c,
        lv_job_start_sofort TYPE c,
        lv_name             TYPE syst-uname, "TR DS1K926242++ Defect 215105 GKOTESWARA 23-Sep-2021
        lv_print_parameters TYPE pri_params,
        lv_group            TYPE btctgtsrvr-srvname.
  lv_job_name = 'ZOTC_R_013_SALES_REP'.
  lv_group = 'SAP_CUSTOM_BTC'.
*Begin of comment TR DS1K926309++ Defect GKOTESWARA 28-Sep-2021
*  lv_name = sy-uname.     "TR DS1K926242++ Defect 215105 GKOTESWARA 23-Sep-2021
*  sy-uname = 'SAPBATCH'.     "TR DS1K926242++ Defect 215105 GKOTESWARA 23-Sep-2021
*End of comment TR DS1K926309++ Defect GKOTESWARA 28-Sep-202
  CALL FUNCTION 'JOB_OPEN'
    EXPORTING
      jobname          = lv_job_name
    IMPORTING
      jobcount         = lv_job_nr
    EXCEPTIONS
      cant_create_job  = 1
      invalid_job_data = 2
      jobname_missing  = 3
      OTHERS           = 4. ##FM_SUBRC_OK
  IF syst-subrc = 0.

*submit job with all the selection screen params...
    SUBMIT (lv_job_name)
        WITH rb_list = abap_false
        WITH rb_email = abap_true
        WITH s_email IN s_email
        WITH bukrs IN bukrs   " parmeters of the bg pgm
        WITH vkorg IN vkorg
        WITH vtweg IN vtweg
        WITH spart IN spart
        WITH fkart IN fkart
        WITH vbeln IN vbeln
        WITH bstnk_vf IN bstnk_vf
        WITH xblnr IN xblnr
        WITH auart IN auart
        WITH fkdat IN fkdat
        WITH ernam IN ernam
        WITH erdat IN erdat
        WITH kunrg IN kunrg
        WITH vbund IN vbund
        WITH land1 IN land1
        USER syst-uname          " mandatory     "TR DS1K926309++ Defect GKOTESWARA 28-Sep-2021
*        USER  sy-uname            " mandatory   "TR DS1K926242++ Defect 215105 GKOTESWARA 23-Sep-2021
        VIA JOB lv_job_name NUMBER lv_job_nr AND RETURN.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      CALL FUNCTION 'JOB_CLOSE'
        EXPORTING
          jobcount             = lv_job_nr
          jobname              = lv_job_name
          strtimmed            = 'X'
          targetserver         = lv_group
        IMPORTING
          job_was_released     = lv_job_released
        EXCEPTIONS
          cant_start_immediate = 1
          invalid_startdate    = 2
          jobname_missing      = 3
          job_close_failed     = 4
          job_nosteps          = 5
          job_notex            = 6
          lock_failed          = 7
          OTHERS               = 8. ##FM_SUBRC_OK
      IF syst-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE i162(00) WITH
        'An error occured while closing the background job.'(099).
        STOP.
      ELSE.
        MESSAGE s022(so).
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
*    sy-uname = lv_name. "TR DS1K926242++ Defect 215105 GKOTESWARA 23-Sep-2021 "TR DS1K926309-- Defect GKOTESWARA 28-Sep-2021
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form select_layout
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      <-- P_LAYOUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_layout  CHANGING p_p_layout.

  DATA: lwa_layout TYPE disvariant,
        l_exit     TYPE char1.

  lwa_layout-report = sy-repid.
  CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_VARIANT_F4'
    EXPORTING
      is_variant = lwa_layout
      i_save     = 'A'
    IMPORTING
      e_exit     = l_exit
      es_variant = lwa_layout
    EXCEPTIONS
      not_found  = 2.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    IF l_exit EQ space.
      p_p_layout = lwa_layout-variant.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
* Begin of change DS1K933937 Defect#TEWA SKUMAR 18-Apr-2023
FORM user_command USING r_ucomm LIKE sy-ucomm
                  rs_selfield TYPE slis_selfield.
  DATA: lv_year  TYPE t009b-bdatj,                        " ++MOD-001
        lv_periv TYPE t009b-periv.                        " ++MOD-001
* Check function code
  CASE r_ucomm.
    WHEN '&IC1'.
*   Check field clicked on within ALVgrid report
      IF rs_selfield-fieldname = 'INVOICE'.
*     Read data table, using index of row user clicked on
        READ TABLE gt_final INTO DATA(lw_final) INDEX rs_selfield-tabindex.
*     Set parameter ID for transaction screen field
        SET PARAMETER ID 'VF' FIELD lw_final-invoice.
*     Execute transaction VF03, and skip initial data entry screen
        CALL TRANSACTION 'VF03' AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
*********************** BEGIN OF INSERT (MOD-001) **************************
      IF rs_selfield-fieldname = 'BELNR'.
        CLEAR: lw_final,lv_periv,lv_year.
        READ TABLE gt_final INTO lw_final INDEX rs_selfield-tabindex.
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
* Get the Physical year variant
          SELECT SINGLE periv
            FROM t001
            INTO lv_periv
            WHERE bukrs = lw_final-vkorg.
* Get the year based on posting date
          CALL FUNCTION 'DATE_TO_PERIOD_CONVERT'
            EXPORTING
              i_date  = lw_final-budat
              i_periv = lv_periv
            IMPORTING
              e_gjahr = lv_year.

          SET PARAMETER ID 'BLN' FIELD lw_final-belnr.
          SET PARAMETER ID 'GJR' FIELD lv_year.
          SET PARAMETER ID 'BUK' FIELD lw_final-vkorg.
          CALL TRANSACTION 'FB03' AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
*********************** END OF INSERT (MOD-001) ****************************
  ENDCASE.
ENDFORM.
* End of change DS1K933937 Defect#TEWA SKUMAR 18-Apr-2023
